Rook at d8 does NOT attack f7.
So f8d6+ Kf7.

What if the rook moves first?
d8d6+

- d5: Queen f8 (diagonal f8-e7-d6-c5-b4-a3. d5 is NOT on this diagonal. Wait. f8-e7-d6 is diagonal. d6-c5 is diagonal. So d5 is NOT on the diagonal f8-d6.

Pawn at e5 captures at f6 or d6. Not f5.

No.
